拉美市場：巴西總統大選前料將震盪，後市中性看待
5 月以來拉美股市表現相對疲弱，主因隨著 10 月巴西總統大選逐步逼近，選情不確定性升溫，
導致最大經濟體巴西股市出現震盪，同時美伊和談狀況膠著，油價持續維持高檔，帶動整體拉美國家
通膨預期升溫，降息預期縮窄，股市上漲動能便受到壓抑。
回顧歷史，巴西股市於大選前半年走勢普遍不佳，過去五次選舉前六個月MSCI 巴西指數報酬中
位數為負 4%，其中 2014 年與 2022 年兩次大選年更以全年下跌作收，顯示選情雜音升高之際，投
資人多於投票前轉趨觀望。在大選過後，巴西股市報酬率則取決於是否由資本市場偏好的候選人當

選，若是，則股市大多上漲，若否，則股市大多下跌，巴西股市 5 月來的跌幅就與此相關。
本次巴西總統大選中，相對受到資本市場偏好的候選人波索納洛，近期捲入醜聞，導致其支持度
大幅滑落，其被指控與涉案巴西史上最大銀行詐欺案 Banco Master 的前執行長關係密切，並涉及收
受其資金用於拍攝家族紀錄片。受此影響，民調機構 AtlasIntel 5 月 19 日最新民調顯示，在第二輪
對決調查中，魯拉以 48.9%支持度領先波索納洛的 41.8%，波索納洛由原先小幅領先轉為落後約 7
個百分點。由於市場對偏左派的魯拉財政與政策路線疑慮較深，民調再度倒向魯拉，連帶導致巴西股
市表現承壓。
另一方面，受美伊戰爭影響，西德州原油價格持續維持高檔，帶動巴西輸入性通膨壓力上升，市
場對通膨回落的信心隨之轉弱。根據巴西央行截至 5 月 22 日的 Focus 市場調查，市場預期 2026 年
消費者物價指數預期中位數已由四週前的 4.86% 升至 5.04%，進一步突破 4.50%的通膨目標上緣；
受此影響，市場對 2026 年底 Selic 基準利率的預期亦由四週前的 13.00%上修至 13.25%，意味市場

預期下半年降息幅度僅約 1 個百分點，降息預期幅度持續收窄，連帶限制股市評價向上修正的空間。
然而儘管受到選舉及通膨升溫兩股承壓力量，高盛最新報告仍對於巴西股市中長線維持加碼評
等，看多理由在於，巴西股市對本地利率的負相關程度為新興市場中之最，在過去降息循環中，股市
於首次降息後往往出現強勁漲勢，而目前巴西市場仍處於降息循環中；此外，今年以來外資對巴西股
市淨流入居新興市場之冠，亦有利股市支撐。
展望後市，降息循環有利巴西股市支撐，然而考量巴西股市於大選前表現普遍不佳，同時油價高
檔推升通膨預期、降息幅度持續收窄的情況下，指數上檔空間恐受壓抑，故我們對於拉美市場後市目
前抱持中性看法。

能源類股：雖仍供不應求，惟油價料將隨談判進度震盪走低
當前油價與能源類股表現仍取決於美伊和談進展， 5 月 23 日川普宣稱一份結束戰事並重啟荷
莫茲海峽的備忘錄已大致談定，將採先確立大方向原則，後續再就細節分軌協商的模式來結束戰爭，
伊朗官媒指出，荷莫茲海峽通行與核子相關議題，將分別在備忘錄簽署後 30 天及 60 天進行談判，
談判期間美國將分階段豁免伊朗制裁。然而截至最新消息，美伊雙方在荷莫茲海峽主權與通行收費、
鈾濃縮是否完全停止等核心議題上仍存在明顯分歧，故該份備忘錄能否順利簽署仍具不確定性。
受到荷莫茲海峽持續封鎖影響，IEA（國際能源署）5 月月報指出，4 月全球石油供給量再度下

降每日 180 萬桶至每日 9,510 萬桶，下降主因波斯灣國家停產量持續上升，不過美國、巴西、加
拿大等國家持續增加出口，也為全球油市提供部分緩解，IEA 將 2026 年美洲供給成長上修逾每日
60 萬桶至平均每日 150 萬桶，大西洋盆地原油出口也自 2 月以來增加每日 350 萬桶。
此外全球石油需求量下滑，也部分抵銷供給缺口。IEA 將 2026 年全球石油需求下修至年減每
日 42 萬桶，較開戰前的預測減少了每日 130 萬桶，產業面看以石化與航空產業需求下滑幅度最
深，國家別中，中國 4 月海運原油進口量相較 2 月大幅減少每日 360 萬桶，日韓印亦分別縮減每日
190 萬、100 萬與 76 萬桶。全球石油需求下降，主因中東石油供給受阻，導致部分石化公司被迫
減產，此外部分也跟中國經濟表現疲弱，導致的需求下滑有關。
然而儘管全球石油需求量下滑 ， 非中東地區國家也持續增產 ， 全球石油市場仍面臨每日約750 萬
桶的供給缺口，受此影響， IEA 統計全球可見庫存 3 月減少 1.29 億桶，4 月再減 1.17 億桶，

高盛則預估 5 月以來全球可見石油庫存以每日 870 萬桶的創紀錄速度被消耗。
但值得注意的是，儘管全球石油市場面臨嚴重供不應求，理應有利油價上漲，然而整個 5 月西
德州原油價格卻仍維持在每桶 80 至 110 美元區間震盪，並未進一步上漲。高盛指出，油價上漲受
阻，主因經濟合作暨發展組織(OECD)國家以每日 130 萬桶速度釋出戰略石油儲備，填補了部分缺
口，同時中國原油進口量自 2 月 27 日以來大減每日 430 萬桶，使市場上對於可售原油的爭奪趨
緩，連帶使油價漲幅受到受抑。
展望後市油價，EIA（美國能源資訊署）預估布蘭特原油價格 5、6 月均價將約為 106 美元，
隨後續海峽通行恢復，第四季油價可望回落至每桶 89 美元，惟若海峽重開延後至六月底，則後續
油價將可能較此預測在上升每桶 20 美元。整體而言，後市油價及能源類股表現，仍將視美伊談判
進度而定，然而考量美伊戰事持續朝收斂方向前進，預料後續油價將隨談判進度震盪走低，相較不利
能源類股表現，在缺乏進一步利多支撐的狀況下，我們對於能源類股後市目前保守看待。

黃金類股：黃金表現仍承壓，惟全球央行買盤漸強
5 月受到美伊戰爭帶動美債殖利率上行影響，黃金表現仍承壓，然而我們觀察到來自全球央行的
買盤逐漸轉為強勁，顯示各國央行對於逢低增持黃金的興趣仍存，有利為金價帶來支撐，預料來自央
行的買盤將可使金價保持在區間內震盪整理，等待美伊衝突緩解，殖利率上行的壓力消退。
央行買盤方面，高盛於5 月報告大幅上修其央行購金量預估，主因其推估模型自 2025 年 8 月起
系統性低估各國央行購金量，經參數修正後，高盛預估各國央行購金量 12 個月移動平均值由原本估
計的每月 29 公噸調升至每月 50 公噸，單月修正幅度更大，1 月由 12 公噸上修至 66 公噸、2 月由 2

公噸上修至 41 公噸，2026 全年央行購金量則預估為平均每月 60 公噸。
世界黃金協會數據亦呼應此預估，根據世界黃金協會統計 ，2026 年第 1 季全球央行淨購金達 244
公噸，季增 17%，超越前季與五年均值。在已申報買方中，波蘭央行 Q1 增持 31 公噸為最大買家，
烏茲別克央行則增持 25 公噸、哈薩克央行 12 公噸緊隨其後。此外中國人行持續增持黃金動作也未
停歇，在 4 月中國人行單月增持 8 公噸黃金，創下 2024 年 12 月以來單月新高，並且是連續第 18
個月增持黃金儲備，帶動其官方持有量上升至 2,322 公噸，占外匯儲備 9%。從以上數據可以看出，
各國央行在近幾個月金價走疲的階段，仍持續加大力度增持黃金儲備。
在黃金 ETF 資金面亦可見到轉折出現的跡象，繼 3 月黃金 ETF 出現顯著資金流出後，4 月全球
實物黃金 ETF 轉為出現 66 億美元的資金流入，所有地區在均呈正向流入，推動全球黃金 ETF 黃金持
有量從 3 月底約 4,092 公噸反彈 45 公噸至 4,137 公噸，達到歷史第三高 ， 僅略低於2/27 創下的 4,176

公噸紀錄，其中歐洲基金表現最強，4 月流入 37 億美元使年初至今累計由負轉正，主要受地緣政治
風險升溫、英國央行立場不如預期鷹派及本地股市回跌驅動。亞洲則延續第 8 個月淨流入趨勢，4 月
增加 18 億美元，其中香港受惠於新 ETF 產品掛牌上市錄得 7.32 億美元單月歷史新高，中國在地緣
緊張 、 殖利率下行及官方持續宣布增持黃金的背景下亦流入 4.98 億美元。由黃金 ETF 數據亦可看出，
市場資金對於逢低增持黃金配置的興趣正在上升。
展望後市，在國際地緣政治紛擾不斷的狀況下 ，仍可預期央行買盤與個人投資組合中分散風險的
需求可望延續，有利為金價帶來支撐，然而在高油價將可能使利率維持在高檔較久的環境下，黃金
ETF 資金流入恐難以重現 2025 年的強度，故預期中短線上金價將可能持續維持在區間內震盪，惟若
後續美伊談判推展順利，隨荷莫茲海峽開放，油價回落將有利市場降息預期回升，屆時搭配來自央行
的買盤，將有望再度重啟黃金新一輪多頭走勢。

### Page 10

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：市場觀望華許首秀，美債利率料陷整理走勢
自 2/28 美伊戰事爆發並導致荷莫茲海峽封鎖以來 ， 全球金融市場經歷自 1970 年代以來最嚴重
的供給端通膨衝擊。西德州原油與布蘭特原油價格的大幅飆升，引發市場對於通膨，乃至於 Fed 利
率政策方向轉變的疑慮，成為推升美債殖利率的核心驅動引擎。
這場由地緣政治引發的能源危機，並非僅侷限於美國單一國家，而是全球公債市場的普遍現象。
由於全球能源報價以美元計價 ， 且原油實體供給短缺屬於全球性危機 ， 主要已開發國家的公債殖利率
呈現極高相關性的同步上揚 ， 其中又以日本與英國的公債殖利率升幅最大 。 日本 75% 能源依賴中東

供給，不論在物價上或是財政體質上面臨不小壓力。
我們預期 6 月市場的總經焦點將在華許首次主持的 6/17 FOMC 利率會議。4 月下旬在分析華許
在參議院聽證會的發言時 ， 便已提出其主張可能導致 「美債殖利率陡峭化」 與 「債市波動性恐將加大」
的想法。彭博 4/30~5/13 調查顯示，118 名受訪者中有 64% 預期華許上任後，美債利率將出現某
種形式的陡峭化，其中熊市陡峭化（長率升速>短率升速）是主流觀點(佔比近 30%)。未來若債券殖
利率曲線呈現陡峭化(不論是熊市、牛市或是扭轉陡峭化)，短中天期的債券殖利率表現都將相對優於
長天期債券，加上短天期債券的存續期間本就低於長天期公債，因此當債市震盪時，短天期債券價格
表現將優於長天期債券。
巴克萊近期研究也顯示，當聯邦基金利率在 2-5% 的區間內波動，平均約為 3.6% 時，30 年
期公債殖利率與聯邦基金利率之間的利差為 170 個基點。假設長期中性利率在 3-4%的區間內（如
聯準會點陣圖所示），30 年期公債殖利率應在 4.7-5.7%區間。而目前約 5%水準約在區間相對偏低

的水準。巴克萊認為，在美國經濟韌性的背景下，目前30 年期公債殖利率還不算高，考慮到當前的
財政狀況和異常的不確定性，投資人可能需要更高的期限溢酬。若此說法成真，相較於短期債券，長
天期債券殖利率上升幅度更高(價格下跌幅度更大)。
雖然 5 月至今股市有所表現，但在中東地緣政治談判反覆，能源通膨預期以及聯準會偏鷹派立場
的交互牽引下，債券市場表現卻相對震盪，30 年期美債殖利率甚至一度觸及 2007 年以來的高位。
近來市場定價邏輯高度聚焦於「通膨風險與聯準會政策路徑」 ，債券殖利率 6 月仍將持續面臨高檔震
盪的壓力。目前債券殖利率水準明顯高於其歷史均值，我們認為站在息收 (Carry)的角度，債券仍具
長線投資價值。此外，由於預期美債殖利率曲線趨向陡峭化，再加上長天期公債本就有較高的存續期
間，故就債券價格來看，短天期公債/投資級債券的表現料將優於長天期公債/投資級債券，因此我們
仍維持債券投資聚焦中短天期公債/投資級債的建議。

與 Amkor (AMKR US) 也 簽 約 相 似 的 預 付 款 合 約。顯 示 AI 晶 片 由 傳 統 單 一 系 統 單 晶 片
(SoC) 朝 Chiplet 及 異 質 整 合 發 展 ，封 測 複 雜 度 提 升 ，IC 設 計 廠 和 封 測 廠 合 作 深 化，

價合理 。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw  
京 元 電 宣 布 與 NVIDIA 簽 訂 預 付 款 協 議 ， 支 持 美 國 全 資 子 公 司 AeternZ 於
當 地 建 置 產 能，但 現 階 段 仍 未 揭 露 合 作 金 額：  
10/13/2026 京 元 電 重 訊 公 告 ， 董 事 會 決 議 通 過 與NVIDIA(NVDA US) 簽 訂 客
戶 預 付 款 協 議，將 由 NVIDIA 預 先 支 付 款 項，支 持 京 元 電 及 美 國 全 資 子 公 司
AeternZ Semiconductor Testing Corporation 在 美 國 建 置 測 試 產 能 ， 未 來 再 以
提 供 測 試 服 務 抵 減 預 付 款。京 元 電 同 步 宣 布，因 合 約 架 構 調 整，取 消 原 先 對
美 國 子 公 司 提 供 的 3 億 美 元 背 書 保 證 額 度 。 
京 元 電 此 次簽約 對 象 為 NVIDIA International, Inc. ， NVIDIA 預 付 款 項 ， 京
元 電 及 其 美 國 全 資 子 公 司 將 在 美 國 建 置 測 試 產 能 ， 為 NVIDIA 保 留 產 能 並
提 供 測 試 服 務，預 付 款 則 以 未 來 服 務 款 項 抵 減。此 合 作 並 非 股 權 投 資 或 合 資
模 式，而 是 在 既 有 客 戶 關 係 下，透 過 客 戶 提 前 提 供 資 金，支 持 供 應 商 擴 充 產
能 並 確 保 未 來 測 試 資 源 。 
京 元 電 董 事 會 已 於 07/ 2026 決 議 通 過 美 國 投 資 設 廠 案，規 劃 在 美 國 設 立 孫 公
司 並 興 建 新 廠 ， 投 資 金 額 以 14 億美元 (約 新 台 幣 450 億元 )為 上 限 。 公 司 當
時 表 示，此 投 資 主 要 因 應 未 來 營 運 需 求 及 全 球 供 應 鏈 布 局，後 續 將 依 設 廠 進
度 與 資 金 需 求 逐 步 投 入 ， 資 金 來 源 以 自 有 資 金 或 借 款 等 方 式 支 應 。 
此 次 協 議 有 助 於 提 高 新 增 產 能 的 需 求 能 見 度，並 降 低 部 分 初 期 資 金 壓 力。不
過，由 於 預 付 款 金 額、實 際 保 留 產 能 規 模 及 相 關 訂 單 金 額 尚 未 公 開，現 階 段
仍 無 法 量 化 對 營 收 與 獲 利 的 貢 獻；短 期 營 運 表 現 仍 取 決 於 既 有 產 能 擴 充、新
晶 片 測 試 需 求 及 美 國 廠 建 置 進 度 。 
NVIDIA 在 07/2026 已和 Amkor(AMKR US) 簽 訂 預 付 款 協 議 ， 約 15 億美
元 ：  
值 得 注 意 的 是 ，NVIDIA 於 07/ 2026 與 Amkor 簽訂約 15 億 美 元 的 多 年 期 先
進 封 裝 與 測 試 合 作 協 議，包 括 正 在 亞 利 桑 那 州 建 設 的 生 產 設 施。Amkor 目前
已為 NVIDIA 資 料 中 心 處 理 器 、 網 通 晶 片 組 及 加 速 運 算 系 統 提 供 先 進 封 裝
服務 。  
Amkor 於 2Q 26 法 說 會 表 示 ， 相 關 預 付 款 預 計 於 2027 年 收 取 ， 並 隨 後 續 提
供 服 務 逐 步 抵 減 ， 依 實 際 生 產 規 模 及 服 務 提 供 速 度 ， 預 估 合 作 期 間 約5~10
年 。 此 案 例 顯 示 ，NVIDIA 透 過 預 付 款 支 持 封 測 廠 擴 充 美 國 產 能 ， 並 非 短 期
採 購 安 排 ， 而 可 能 是 配 合 長 期 產 能 建 置 及 技 術 發 展 的 合 作 模 式 。 
京 元 電 此 次 亦 採 取和 Amkor 相 似 的 方 式 ，客 戶 預 付 款 、 建 置 美 國 產 能 並 以
未 來 測 試 服 務 款 項 抵 減。 Amkor 案 例 可 作 為 評 估 合 作 期 間 的 參 考。  
2 家 不 同 之 處，京 元 電 主 要 受 惠 於 高 階 測 試 需 求；Amkor 則 涉 及 先 進 封 裝 與

測 試。兩 者 都 能 提 高 產 能 需 求 能 見 度，但 實 際 營 收 與 獲 利 貢 獻 仍 取 決 於 產 能
開 出 、 訂 單 規 模 及 服 務 條 件 。 
外 媒 報 導 京 元 電 美 國 子 公 司預 計 落 腳 德 州 沃 斯 堡 市(Fort Worth )：  
在 美 國 設 廠 地 點 方 面， 外 媒 於10/ 2026 報 導， 京 元 電 全 資 美 國 子 公 司AeternZ
正 評 估 於 德 州 沃 斯 堡(Fort Worth )設 立 半 導 體 測 試 廠，初 步 規 劃 投 資 約 13 億
美 元 ， 其 中 約 10 億 美 元 用 於 不 動 產 、3 億 美 元 用 於 設 備 ， 選 址 涉 及 West 
Everman Parkway 一 處 約 23 英 畝 的 土 地 。 沃 斯 堡 市 議 會 預 計 於 10 /27/2026
審 議 相 關 租 稅 優 惠，但 目 前 尚 不 能 視 為 京 元 電 已 正 式 決 定 設 廠 地 點，仍 須 觀
察 後 續 選 址 及 投 資 進 度。若 該 地 點 獲 選，德州相對 台 積 電 所 在 的 亞 利 桑 那 州
鳳 凰 城 聚 落 ，地 緣 上 相 對 鄰 近 AI 伺 服 器 供 應 鏈 ，代 表 AI 晶 片 完 成 測 試 後，
可 就 近 送 往 當 地 代 工 廠 ， 進 行 整 機 組 裝 與 系 統 測 試 。不 過 ， 廠 房 建 置 、 設 備
進 駐 及 客 戶 認 證 仍 需 時 間 ， 實 際 貢 獻 時 點 取 決 於 建 廠 進 度 與 測 試 需 求 。 
AI 晶 片 由 傳 統 單 一 系 統 單 晶 片(SoC) 朝 Chiplet 及 異 質 整 合 發 展， 封 測 複 雜
度 提 升 ， IC 設 計 廠 和 封 測 廠 合 作 深 化：  
隨著 AI 晶 片 由 傳 統 單 一 系 統 單 晶 片(SoC ) 朝 Chiplet 及 異 質 整 合 發 展 ， 半 導
體 產 業 的 競 爭 已 不 再 侷 限 於 晶 片 設 計 與 先 進 製 程，也 延 伸 至 先 進 封 裝、晶 粒
間 高 速 互 連、散 熱 及 測 試 驗 證 等 環 節。晶 片 架 構 複 雜 度 提 高，使 封 裝 與 測 試
成 為 影 響 產 品 效 能 、 成 本 及 量 產 能 力 的 重 要 因 素 。 
對 京 元 電 而 言，若 能 持 續 參 與 NVIDIA 新 晶 片 的 測 試 開 發 與 驗 證，並 承 接 後
續 量 產 測 試，有 助 於 深 化 客 戶 關 係 及 鞏 固 高 階 測 試 業 務 的 競 爭 優 勢；但 此 次
預付款協議本身並未改變雙方的股權關係，合作價值仍須透過後續訂單與
產 能 利 用 率 驗 證 。 
相 較 之 下 ， 力 成(6239 TT) 與 Broadcom (AVGO US) 規 劃 在 新 加 坡 成 立 先 進 封
裝 合 資 公 司 ， 發 展 扇 出 型 面 板 級 封 裝 （FOPLP ）及 重 布 線 層 （RDL ） 等 相 關
技術，屬於 IC 設 計 公 司 直 接 參 與 封 裝 製 造 平 台 投 資 的 模 式 。 此 合 作 不 僅 涉
及 產 能 配 置，也 涵 蓋 新 技 術 開 發 與 量 產 導 入，反 映 當 先 進 封 裝 成 為 影 響 晶 片
效 能 、 互 連 密 度 及 量 產 成 本 的 關 鍵 環 節 ，IC 設 計 公 司 可 能 更 積 極 參 與 封 裝
技 術 與 產 能 布 局。對 力 成 而 言，若 能 順 利 完 成 製 程 驗 證、良 率 提 升 及 量 產 導
入，有 機 會 拓 展 新 的 技 術 平 台 與 業 務 成 長 機 會；惟 仍 須 觀 察 客 戶 訂 單、量 產
進 度 、 資 本 支 出 及 投 資 報 酬 率 。 
整 體 而 言 ，NVIDIA 與 京 元 電 、Amkor 的 合 作 ， 主 要 是 透 過 客 戶 預 付 款 支 持
產 能 建 置 ， 強 化 供 應 商 的 需 求 與 資 金 能 見 度 ；Broadcom 與 力 成 的 合 作 ， 則
是 透 過 共 同 投 資 發 展 新 的 先 進 封 裝 技 術 平 台 。 兩 者 反 映AI 半 導 體 產 業 的 合
作 模 式 正 逐 步 深 化，但 資 金 安 排、技 術 參 與 程 度 及 投 資 風 險 並 不 相 同。投 資
上，京 元 電 應 關 注 預 付 款 規 模、美 國 新 廠 投 產 及 高 階 測 試 訂 單 成 長；力 成 則
應 觀 察 先 進 封 裝 技 術 商 業 化、量 產 良 率 與 合 資 事 業 的 投 資 報 酬。京 元 電 此 次
協 議 對 中 長 期 業 務 發 展 具 正 面 意 義，但 在 實 際 財 務 貢 獻 尚 未 明 朗 前，宜 視 為
客 戶 關 係 深 化 及 產 能 布 局 的 正 面 訊 號，而 非 直 接 上 修 獲 利 預 估 的 依 據。由於
目 前 京 元 電 評 價 合 理 ，投 資 建 議 Neutral 。

4 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Lite-On Technology  | Asia Pacific
September  Sales -4% MoM/
+22% YoY
In this report, we focus on Lite-on's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• September sales were NT$18,848mn (-4% MoM/+22% YoY).
• 3Q26 sales came in at NT$57,446mn (+9% QoQ/+28% YoY), lower than our
estimate of NT$59,526mn (+13% QoQ/+33% YoY) and  the consensus at NT
$60,259mn (+14% QoQ/+34% YoY).
• By segment, Cloud&AIoT/Opto-electronics/IT&Consumer Electronics

accounted for 59%/14%/27% of total sales, respectively. This implies growth
of -5%/-4%/flat MoM and  +53%/+7%/-11% YoY for each segment,
respectively, in the month.
Our view:
• We believe softer revenue growth in September primarily reflected
component shortages at customers in the cloud segment and, to a lesser
extent, the consumer electronics segment.
• Looking ahead, we expect 4Q26 revenue to grow by double digits QoQ,
driven mainly by the cloud segment. We expect gross margin to normalise to

around 25% in 2H26, versus 27.2% in 2Q26, as inventory write-back benefits
fade and shipments of high-margin products delayed from 2Q26 flow
through.
• Stay EW.

12-18 months.

the next 12-18 months.

12-18 months.

## Document 214: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가HD현대중공업(329180) 드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가드디어 엔진 증설, 저평_dc1151a4

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가HD현대중공업(329180) 드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가드디어 엔진 증설, 저평_dc1151a4.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-11
Pages: 4

### Page 1

2026.09.11  
HD 현대중공업 (329180)

드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가  Flash Note 
[조선/기계/방산] 변용진 2122-9181 yongjin.byun@imfnsec.com

Check Point  Buy (Maintain) 
HD현대중공업 중형엔진 및 SMR 공장 증설 결정  
엔진도 끌어올리지 못하는 조선업 주가, 그래도 펀더멘털은 굳건

목표주가(12M) 860,000 원(유지) 
종가(2026.09.10) 453,500 원 
상승여력 89.6 %

HD현대중공업, 중형엔진 및 SMR 시설투자 공시  
기다리던 엔진 증설의 세부 사항이 드디어 공개되었다. HD 현대중공업은 10일 공시를 통해 총 1조 722억원(중형엔진 
8,336억원/SMR 2,386억원) 규모의 시설투자 계획을 밝혔다. 먼저 중형엔진 관련, 글로벌 전력 수요 증가에 따른 엔진발전 시장 
확대에 대응 목적으로 생산능력 확충을 위한 토지 취득 및 공장 신설 및 생산설비 구축에 28년 5월까지 투자가 진행될 예정이다. 
중형엔진의 생산 능력은 현재 연간 3GW급에서 증설이 완료되면 7.2GW급으로 확장되며, 첫 엔진 출하는 28년 하반기로 예상된다. 
증설되는 신공장의 가동률은 28년 30%로 출발하여 30년 100%로 올라설 전망이다. 이에 따라 연간 1GW당 약 1조원의 매출과 
20%이상의 영업이익(현 엔진사업부 이상) 수준으로 추정되며, 계획대로 증설이 완료되고 일감을 확보한다면 총 4조원 이상의 매출 
증대 효과가 기대된다. SMR 투자 관련해서는 SMR 주기기 제작사업 진입 및 수주물량 증가에 대응하기 위해 SMR 전용 공장, 설비 
구축 및 기존 공장 최적화에 29년 4월까지 2,386억원이 투입된다. 이는 연간으로 Terrapower의 Natrium SMR 주기기 2기(각 
345MW)를 제작할 수 있는 규모다. HD현대중공업은 이번 증설이 데이터센터용 엔진만을 위한 것이 아님을 강조했다. 데이터센터 뿐 
아니라 국가전력망, 원전용 비상발전기 등 전세계적으로 증가하는 육상용 발전엔진 수요에 대응하기 위함이며, 최근 미국의 육상 
데이터센터 사업이 님비현상 등으로 일부 좌초되고 있음에도 그보다 더 큰 그림에 베팅한 것이다.  
엔진도 끌어올리지 못하는 조선업 주가, 그래도 펀더멘털은 굳건  
분명한 기업가치 증분의 근거가 되는 증설공시였음에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업의 주가는 발표 이후 하락으로 마감하였다. 올해 
조선업 주가는 YTD 평균 10%이상 하락하며 약세를 보이고 있는 상태다. (HD현대중공업 -10.0%, 삼성중공업 -11.6%, 한화오션 -
25.5%)  
조선사 공히 2분기의 실적 서프라이즈와 함께 올해 수주목표를 사실상 초과 달성하는 등 우수한 펀더멘털을 유지하고 있으나 이는 
주가 상승재료로 반영되고 있지 않다. 7~8월 반도체발 주식시장 혼란에 따른 전체적 수급 약세도 무시할 수 없는 요인이다. 여기에 
지난해 주가 상승의 주요 동력으로 작용하였던 미국 함정 사업 관련 협력 진전이 느린 점 등이 주가 약세의 주요 원인으로 
분석된다. 백악관 및 美해군에서는 지속적으로 함정 해외 건조의 필요성을 역설하고 있으나 美입법부에서는 지역구 의원의 반대 
등으로 관련 법안의 입법 동력이 약해져 있는 상황이다. 美국회의 이번 회기(119대 회기, ~2027년 1월 3일까지)가 이미 막바지에 
다다르고 있으며 11월 중간선거 일정까지 감안하면 실질적으로 이번 회기중에 관련 법안이 통과 및 발효될 가능성은 미미하기 
때문에, 2026년 남은 하반기에 함정사업 관련 조선업 주가 동력을 기대하기는 힘들어졌다. 다가오고 있는 3분기 실적도 원화 강세 
및 여름휴가/사고 등에 따른 조업일수 하락, 후판 유통가 상승 등 감익 요인이 산적해 있어 2분기에 비해 쉽지 않은 상황이다.  
그러나 여전히 본업인 조선업의 체력은 훌륭하다. 전세계 선박 발주량은 8월 누적기준 5,972만 CGT로 전년동기 대비 +60.6% 많은 
발주가 나오고 있으며 이는 2021년 이후 가장 강한 발주세다. 한국 조선사의 주력 선종인 LNG, 탱커, 컨테이너선의 발주 비중이 
전체 발주량의 67%를 차지하고 있으며 이에 힘입어 조선사들은 수주목표를 사실상 초과 달성하였다. (상선기준, HD현대중공업 달성 
130.6억 달러/목표 114.7억, 삼성중공업 달성 61억/목표 57억, 한화오션 달성 60억/목표없음) 3분기 실적은 다소 쉬어갈 수 
있으나 여전히 수주잔고에 기반한 추정상 매출/영업이익은 2028년까지 꾸준한 상승이 예상된다. 작년보다 오히려 하락한 주가가 
현저한 저평가일 수밖에 없는 이유다. 조선업 비중확대 및 HD현대중공업 Top Pick 의견을 유지한다.

### Page 2

HD 현대중공업 (329180) 
2 
표1. 26.09.10. HD현대중공업 발전엔진·SMR 신규 시설투자 공시 
구분 발전엔진 생산 SMR 전용 공장

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급  
시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천하는 것임 
 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

D

Vietnam’s economic success story has continued
 , 
despite last year’s US tariff shock and this year’s energy shock  
◼ 
 Economic growth accelerated to 8% in 2025 and 8.2% y/y in H1 2026, driven by exports, investment and private consumption. 
 It is 
projected to slow in the short term, reaching a still strong 
 level of 
 7.3% in 2026. 
 External accounts strengthened in 
 2025
, but 
they 
have 
 deteriorat
 ed 
in recent months. Public and external debt ratios 
 remain  
moderate.  
◼ 
 Vietnam is highly exposed to the global energy shock
 , 
but 
the consequences have been 
 less severe  
than expected  
so far
 . Economic 
activity 
 has continued to grow 
 strong
 ly
, notably because the export
 -
 oriented manufacturing sector 
 has been 
 boosted by global 
demand for AI goods. 
 F
iscal and current account balances can absorb a deterioration
 . 
 I
nflation has increased 
 while 
 currency 
depreciation pressures  
have remained 
 manageable. 
 Yet, 
as 
 the negative impact of high energy prices 
 is expected to persist, 
 V
ietnam  
could face 
 more 
severe external  
liquidity tensions going forward.

 g 
US tariffs
 , notably 
because

 -

S

(+28%). V

 ’s trade balance

19 
bn

2026 
 (customs data)

COUNTRY RISK PROFILE
: STRONG ECONOMIC GROWTH AND LOW PUBLIC 
AND 
FOREIGN DEBT VS. LOW EXTERNAL 
LIQUIDITY AND 
A 
WEAK BANKING SECTOR

◼ 
 Strong factors support 
 Vietnam’s 
 overall country risk  
and economic prospects
 :  
i) 
a 
strong manufacturing export base that is moving up the value chain
 ; 
ii) 
s
olid comparative advantages that continue to attract large FDI inflows
 ; 
iii) 
c
urrent account surpluses and moderate foreign debt
 ; 
iv) 
a 
moderate government debt burden
 ; 
v) 
g
radually improving policy making
 ; 
vi) 
 s
trong medium
 -
 term economic growth prospects, assuming  
 that 
 the authorities will continue to implement structural reforms 
and strengthen productivity in the entire economy.

◼ 
 On the negative side,  
major negative factors continue to fuel some macro
 -
 financial risks:  
i) t
 he excessive domestic debt level of the economy (excluding the government) and fragilities in the banking sector 
 – 
this is a bad 
combination that fuels high credit risks and financial instability risks
 ; 
ii) 
 low forex reserves
 , which 
 have not much strengthened in recent years
 , 
 despite current account surpluses and notably due to 
resident capital flight. Forex reserves do not  
provide good  
protect
ion 
against shocks, while 
 Vietnam 
 is vulnerable to external shocks
 ;  
iii) 
 v
ery high exposure to physical climate risk.  
 C
oastal and river
 -
 delta regions, where population and economic activity are 
concentrated, 
 are 
 highly exposed to sea
 -
 level rise
 s 
and flooding
 .

◼ 
 Non transfer risk and currency risk
 : 
 V
ietnam  
 has enjoyed very 
 favourable  
 external account dynamics in recent years  
 until 2025
 , 
thanks to 
 large FDI inflows
 , 
recurrent 
 current
 -
 account surpluses  
and declining f
 oreign debt (
 which is moderate, at 
 less than  
30% of 
both GDP and exports). 
 This trend 
 has been  
interrupted 
 in 
 2026 
as 
current account surpluses 
 are 
 decreasing
 . Meanwhile
 , 
there is a 
persist
 ent 
 risk of 
 increasing 
 resident capital outflows 
 fuelling  
 some 
 VND 
 depreciation pressure  
 (
w
hereas  
 the potential for foreign 
capital outflows is limited
 )
. 
As 
the forex  
reserve cushion is weak
 , it would 
 not enable the 
 central bank 
 to 
defend the dong for a long 
time 
i
f there is  
severe currency and external liquidity pressure
 . 
However, so far this year, 
 the 
VND/USD spot rate and the reference 
rate 
 (Vietnam has a crawling band FX regime) 
 have depreciated only slightly
 , supported by strong exports and FDI inflows
 .

◼ 
 Sovereign risk
 : 
the government debt burden 
 is moderate and 
 will remain 
 so in the short term, even if the fiscal deficit is projected to 
deteriorate
 . The fiscal deficit could exceed 3% of GDP in 2026 due to support measures in response to the energy shock (estimated at 
less than 1% of GDP in H1 2026), but this will not endanger public
 -
 debt dynamics. Government debt should fall again in 2027 thanks 
to lower deficits and fast GDP growth. Debt was only 30% of GDP in 2025 (vs. 41% in 2019) and interest payments consumed less  
than 5% of fiscal revenue (vs. 7.2% in 2019). The government cover
 s its funding needs mostly in the local bond market, which is 
deepening gradually and not 
 highly 
 exposed to foreign investors. Government debt in foreign currency declined to about 25% of total 
debt in 2025 (vs. 38% in 2019); this is still substantial, but almost all this FC debt 
 is made up  
 of concessional loans from official 
creditors. On the negative front, high credit risks in the state corporate and bank sectors 
 are 
 very large contingent risks for the 
sovereign. Moreover, public investment needs related to climate change and infrastructure development will increase in the 
 medium 
term
.

◼ 
 Systemic credit risk
 : 
Domestic credit to the private sector is too large and still rising (141% of GDP in 2025 vs. 108% in 2019) while 
the banking sector remains poorly capitali
 s
ed. Credit risks and resource misallocation have long been exacerbated by opacity and 
supervision flaws, as well as the use of high credit growth targets to support economic growth (+15% in 2026). In 2022
 –
2023, banks 
faced severe funding stress and asset
 -
 quality deterioration resulting from the property and bond market crisis. The situation has 
recovered gradually since then. In the 
 short term
 , credit risks may increase amid higher energy costs and if  
there is  
a correction in 
the tech cycle. Some banks, especially small private ones, also remain exposed to VND funding risk (whereas banks do not rely  
much 
on 
 foreign
-
 currency 
 financing). Positively, reforms, including stricter capital
 -
 adequacy requirements, continue to be introduced 
gradually 
 in order 
 to improve banks’ creditworthiness (
 the 
 SBV plans to test the removal of credit quotas for the soundest banks 
from 2026).

Oct Edition: Choppy Markets, Intact AI

Overseas

Jeff Pu, CFA 
SFC CE No. BNO719 
jeffpu@gfgroup.com.hk 
Henry Huang 
SFC CE No. BYA749 
henryhuang@gfgroup.com.hk

Market to remain choppy: Tech equities were volatile in the past month, with rotation 
toward hyperscaler and internet names. That said , s emi and memory (Korea) 
weakness reflected lower risk appetite due to rising bond yields, AI monetization/ARR 
visibility and L-T AI debates (i.e., Broadcom CEO warned that capital may now be a 
bigger constraint), even as company-specific catalysts continued to be strong. On the 
other hand, the positive was the acceleration of AI model scaling (i.e., Meta’s Muse 
Wonder, Gemini 4, etc). That said, w e view the pullback primarily as a reset in 
expectations rather than the evidence of AI deterioration. Despite light positioning, we 
expect the markets will remain choppy in the near term but would  accumulate low 
valuation & sustainable names  (i.e., HBM, non-TSMC foundries, CPUs, CPOs, etc) . 
In our preferred list, we added CLS, while removing MTK and Hon Hai.  
U.S. roadshow takeaways (week of Oct 5): Investor tone skewed neutral to slightly 
cautious, driven by conservative top ‑down macro views, U.S. election, and ongoing 
debates around the L-T AI demand. Positioning in AI infrastructure has come off peak 
levels. Even  so, many investors would accumulate on pullbacks, with interest 
concentrated in “Made in U.S.” names (INTC, LITE, COHR), U.S. memory suppliers, 
TSMC, and selective bottleneck themes — including ASE, ABF substrates, 
MRVL/SMTC, and Japanese MLCCs. 
OCP & earnings season in focus: The week is anchored by AI/semis — SEMICON 
West, OCP, and earnings from ASML, TSMC, etc. OCP reinforced the industry’s pivot 
toward rack‑scale infrastructure, with inference and agentic workloads driving demand 
across compute, memory, and networking. Scale‑up optics, NPO/CPO, and 800VDC 
power were key themes, while AMD’s CPU outlook and Helios/Helion underscored a 
secular CPU uptrend. Nvidia ’s use of PTFE for NVLink boards further supports our 
positive stance on PTFE (see PCB Spec in 2 H27, Oct 5).  On earnings, ASML 
delivered largely in -line results, GM and OP above consensus with revenue slightly 
miss, and raised full year guidance again and is exploring ways to increase capacity 
by >30% 2028 to fulfill strong customer demand, also extending the order visibility 
beyond 2028. For TSMC, we anticipate an inline quarter. 
September monthly sales/data: TSMC sales -0.6% MoM, slightly ahead. Rubin to 
accelerate and T3 ramp. PC forecast trimmed. Substrate/CCL upcycle with 
pricing/margin expansion. Aspeed revenue +101% YoY. Details on page 5. 
GFHK Tech Most Read:  1) Jeff Pu on ASIC Updates on TPU v9 ; 2) Michelle Jing 
on PCB Spec in 2H27; 3) Alicia Xia on CPO Acceleration; 4) Jeff Pu on NAND Re-
acceleration; 5) Jeff Pu on TSMC 3Q26 Preview.

Sector Report | Technology

GFHK Tech Important Stock Calls  
In the past one month, our key reports and stock calls are: 
Jeff Pu on ASIC Updates on TPU v9, reported date on Oct 13: We expect near-term MTK headwinds 
from TPU v9 delays but remain constructive on MTK/MRVL longer term. Humufish’s redesign could 
push tape-out to Jan -27, MP tape -out to 2Q27 and shipments to 1Q28, while EMIB -T yields could 
recover from 45–50%. Rising TPU allocations to Anthropic could constrain Gemini compute, though 
higher internal allocation in 2H26 may support future models. SemiAnalysis expects Anthropic/OpenAI 
to each add 5 –10GW in 2027 vs. a few GW for Google. For TPU v10 (4Q28 /1Q29), MTK/GUC’s 
Icefish remains the only confirmed mainstream SKU, while Broadcom-led Merope faces AMD/Marvell 
competition, with both potentially testing EMIB-T. 
Michelle Jing on PCB Spec in 2H27, reported date on Oct 5: Some market watchers expect Nvidia 
to shift from PTFE to hydrocarbon resin (HC) for the VR300 NVL576 switch tray. HC—one of the glass 
free alternatives within PTFE hybrid architectures—has only recently passed SI testing and has yet to 
enter PCB MP, leaving it roughly three months behind PTFE in qualification. On the contrary, we 
believe that PTFE offers superior performance for high frequency, high speed designs. With VR300 
NVL576 shipments expected in 2H27 and the final configuration likely set by end 26 —and with a 
leading Chinese PCB ma ker already achieving meaningful PTFE hybrid yields —we maintain that 
PTFE hybrid remains the most probable solution for next year’s switch board. 
Alicia Xia on CPO Acceleration , reported date on  Sept 29 : Nvidia’s CPO scale -out switch is 
progressing faster than expected, with FAU yields improving. We forecast CPO switch shipments at 
15k/16k/30k in 4Q26/1Q27/2Q27 and 100k in 2027. For Nvidia/AWS scale -up, CPO and NPO 
continue in parallel, with full NPO adoption expected at NVL576 launch. Feynman is also devel oping 
both variants, with CPO potentially using QM6 + six 6.4T OEs. We shift AWS Trainium 4 launch to 
1Q28 and forecast global scale-up OE demand at 6.5m/42m in 2027E/28E. 
Jeff Pu on NAND Re -acceleration, reported date on Sept 20 : According to TrendForce, NAND 
demand is stronger than expected as U.S. CSPs scale AI infrastructure, with eSSD demand nearly 
2x prior forecasts. We raise NAND contract price growth to +21%/+17%/+15% QoQ in 
3Q26/4Q26/1Q27, while hyperscaler inventories decline. Supply remains disci plined at 15%/21%/20% 
YoY bit growth in 2025 –27. Demand is supported by agentic AI, vector databases and KV -cache 
offloading, where eSSD offers lower cost/bit than DRAM and better power efficiency than HDD. 
Jeff Pu on TSMC 3Q26 Preview , reported date on  Oct 7 : TSMC shares hit record highs on 
expectations ahead of the print and potential Terafab collaboration. We expect largely inline 3Q26 
results, capex and full-year guidance, with softer 4Q26 margin (67.2%). Management should reaffirm 
2026 capex at the ceiling and mid-40s YoY growth, while we forecast 37% growth in 2027 on sustained 
AI/CPU/networking demand and price hikes. Key focuses include margins, long -term capex/U.S. 
expansion, large-reticle packaging and A14. We raise 2026E/27E EPS by 2%/8% on hig her N2/N3 
capacity and operating leverage, and roll to 20x 2027E P/E, lifting TP to NT$2,960.

### Page 5

Sector Report | Technology

September Monthly Data  
TSMC September sales of NT$511.9bn, -0.6% MoM and +54.6% YoY, with total 3Q revenue reaching 
NT$1.49T, +17.6 QoQ, slightly ahead of expectations of +14%. The solid performance was well 
supported by ASIC, A20 Pro, and Rubin. For 3Q26, we expect gross margin to be 67.8%, higher than 
guidance of 65-67% and consensus of 66.4% . For 4Q26, we expect another 12% QoQ growth with 
GM at 67.2%, both ahead of street’s estimates of +10% QoQ and 66% margin. Ahead of the earnings 
call, key focus include gross margin trajectory, long -term capex discipline, and the execution of its 
U.S. fab expansion. 
Rubin to accelerate:  Hon Hai revenue last month was NT$1.16T, +26 % MoM, +38% YoY, mainly 
driven by iPhone build.  Quanta and Wistron delivered -23% and -17% MoM respectively, due to AI 
racks’ shipping timing differences. Of note, Pegatron surprised the market with +143.2% MoM revenue 
surge fueled by initial GB300 rack shipments to neocloud customers including Lambda and Together 
AI. This reaffirms our thesis of robust NVL72 order momentum across Neoclouds. Looking ahead, 
VR200 is ramping now, with volume deployment accelerating into 2027. We maintain our forecast of 
75k and 92k NVL72 (GB+VR) rack shipments for 2026 and 2027, respectively. Concurrently, Wiwynn 
delivered another solid month with revenue expanding 20.0% MoM, underpinne d by resilient GP 
server demand alongside the ramp of Trainium 3. 
ASIC ramping up: On design service vendors fronts, driven  by T3 ramp, Alchip’s Sep revenue 
reached a record NT$9.9 8bn. Backed by secured incremental 3nm wafer allocation and improving 
yields, management raised its 2027 revenue guidance, now targeting growth above the previous 50% 
YoY baseline. That said, we push out T4 shipment timeline into 1Q28. GUC delivered a strong beat 
with monthly revenue reaching a record NT$7.145bn (+22.4% MoM, +97.8 % YoY), driven by robust 
Axion 2 CPU demand. Beyond its engagement with top 4 CSPs, multi -year order visibility into 2027 
and beyond is further bolstered by high-margin (likely TK1) automotive ASIC designs. 
PC forecast trimmed: NB shipments from top 5 ODMs were 9.7m in Sep, with 3Q26 shipments down 
17% QoQ and 26% YoY. Global PC OEM shipments contracted 20.1% YoY in 3Q26, worse than 
expected, post robust 1H26 pull -ins with high pricing points and weak macro. For 2027, we project 
the industry will decline a further 5% YoY. On the competitive front, tier -1 vendors such as Lenovo, 
Dell, and HP are not immune to this downturn, while entry-level laptops (low cost models from China) 
continue to see stronger relative demand.  
Substrate/CCL Upcycle Intact: Unimicron continued to deliver another all-time high sales with +5% 
MoM and 65% YoY. Supported by ABF upward pricing of ~15% in 2H26 (likely more next year) and 
higher mix of AI products, we expect a meaningful GM uptick for this quarter with momentum to sustain 
into 2027 . In the meantime, ZengDing’s revenue rose 28% MoM and 36% YoY mainly driven by  
iPhone, 1.6T mSAP, and Rubin’s HDI. On the CCL front, bolstered by ongoing price hikes and strong 
demand from GP servers, ITEC revenue grew 15.5% MoM and 111% YoY, and NanYa Plastics 
recorded +12.5% MoM and +55% YoY. Meanwhile, EMC and TUC both posted another single digit 
growth MoM on sales.

Downstream tech – Smartphone & Apple  
We maintain Global smartphone forecast of -14% in 2026  
According to IDC, the preliminary 2Q26 smartphone data points to a still ‑soft market with uneven 
regional dynamics, and their commentary continues to emphasize structural rather than cyclical 
headwinds. Smartphone shipments remain under pressure from weak replacement cycles, cautious 
consumer spending, and limited breakthrough features. IDC typically highlights that “growth is 
concentrated in the premium tier,” driven by ecosystem lock ‑in, while the mass market remains 
challenged by price sensitivity. They also tend to note that China and parts of emerging markets show 
only modest stabilization, not a full recovery, and that vendors are prioritizing margin discipline over 
volume.  
Overall, by factoring in higher iPhone 17/17 Pro volume, we fine tune our global smartphone shipment 
forecast to -14% YoY from -15% YoY. 
For 2027, we expect global smartphone shipments to be flattish YoY. That said, 2026 -2027 will be 
one of the most challenging periods for the industry, driven primarily by sharply higher memory prices, 
which have already pushed OEMs to reduce shipments and raise device prices.

iPhone 18 Pro/18 PM demand lukewarm  
iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max wait-time lukewarm: iPhone 18 Pro and iPhone 18 Pro Max pre-orders began 
on Sept 12 in the U.S. and many other countries. Generally speaking, the wait-time appears lukewarm, 
with: 1) 3–4 weeks for 256GB/512GB Pro Max across all colors, as well as Burgundy 1TB in the U.S.；
2) 2–3 weeks for Glacier 256GB/512GB Pro Max in China; 3) 1–2 weeks for Black/Glacier 1TB Pro 
Max in the U.S. ；4) immediate availability for Silver 1TB and mos t 2TB Pro Max models in the U.S, 
as well as most iPhone 18 Pro configurations except for Burgundy 256GB in the U.S. We attribute the 
lukewarm wait-time to limited spec upgrade, higher pricing (especially for large storage models), etc. 
We also expect the Pro Max to be impacted by iPhone Duo and the criticism of heavy weight.  
iPhone Duo feedback generally positive 
The iPhone Duo, priced at $1,999 in the U.S., opened pre‑orders on Oct 16 and officially launched on 
Oct 23. Early reviews were positive, highlig hting its polished folding experience, high ‑quality inner 
display, and improved multitasking in iOS 27, but also point to trade ‑offs including a heavier and 
thicker form factor, a visible crease, and a dual ‑camera. Due to hinge constraints, we are reducing  
the iPhone Duo EMS build plan from 7m to 6m units in 2026. We also lower the iPhone 18 Pro/Pro 
Max build plan to 72m units given the constraints associated with the adjustable‑aperture camera.

Sector Report | Technology

Downstream tech – PC 
Global PC units down to -12% in 2026, followed by another decline in 2027 
On Oct 8, IDC reported 3Q26 global PC shipments fell 20.1% YoY, missing street expectations of a 
10–15% decline. The sharper contraction was primarily driven by front -loaded demand in 1H26, 
elevated ASPs, and broader macro weakness. This aligns with our prior view that ODM build 
momentum moderated starting in end -2Q26, amid the rising concerns over channel inventory. 
Meanwhile, the top 5 ODMs also saw 3Q26 shipments decline 26% YoY. Looking ahead, we model 
4Q26 shipments to continue 20% YoY drop (flattish QoQ), underperforming normal seasonality. 
On the component front, we expect memory de -spec to normalize. End -users still require sufficient 
DRAM capacity (16GB+) to handle on -device AI workloads, and PC DDR 5 supply constraints have 
eased compared to Server’s. On pricing, new models will still have higher pricing points to pass 
through 2H component cost increases across memory, CPU, etc. That said, we anticipate price hikes 
will remain measured vs 1H26 given sluggish end-market demand. 
Overall, reflecting weaker than expected 3Q26 results post pull -ins and a muted 4Q outlook, we trim 
our 2026 PC shipment forecast to -12% YoY. For 2027, we project shipments to decline further by 5% 
YoY, as higher ASP and persistent macro headwinds continue to weigh on demand.

manufactured in China continue to gain traction across China and Southeast Asia. Looking ahead, we 
expect value-tier models to remain the primary driver in sustaining ongoing PC shipments.

Upstream tech – AI Semi & Supply Chain  
U.S. CSP capex to be +85%/+53%/+20% in 2026/2027/2028 
Given capex expands further into 2027, CSPs free cash flow are undergoing significant pressure and 
turning negative except Microsoft, such that shifting to debt financing for their massive capex. In 2025, 
Top 5 CSPs’ total debt issuance reached $108bn, or 24% of total capex . Per our Addressing 2028 
Capex and AI Platform Value Shift report on Aug 6, we forecast Top 5 CSPs’ debt issuance to reach 
$257bn/$419bn in 2026/2027, or 31%/35% of total capex.  Assuming debt issuance to grow in 
2028/2029 at a mild pace, we believe U.S. CSPs' balance sheet (except Oracle) to remain very healthy, 
due to net leverage (EBITDA basis) of only 0.5 and the net debt-to-equity ratio of only 18% by 2029.  
Despite healthy balance sheet for most CSPs, we expect most to safeguard balance sheets beyond 
2027 amid widening spreads and FCF profiles, with the focus shifting to returns. Together with a swing 
factor of competition from Chinese LLMs, we expect U.S. CSPs to decelerate capex expansion. We 
forecast Top 5 CSPs capex of $1,512bn/1,739bn in 2028/2029, or +20%/15% YoY, down from +53% 
in 2027.

Server CPU TAM to grow at 49% CAGR by 2030 
As the orchestration core of AI infrastructure buildouts, the Server CPU has gained significant traction 
over the past few months. This is driven by the Agentic AI/inference trend, fueled by the breakthrough 
of OpenClaw and Anthropic’s ARR surging to $44bn  in April. For instance, in latest earnings calls, 
AMD projected a >35% CAGR for Server CPU TAM by 2030, and Intel said the shift toward agentic 
AI is driving a structural demand for CPU, where the GPU-to-CPU ratio is tightening from 8:1 in training 
to 4:1 in inference. Based on our estimates, assuming inference accounts for 90% of AI workloads 
and GPU-to-CPU ratio for AI servers to reach 1:1.2 in 2030, we now expect Server CPU TAM to grow 
96%/71% in 2026/2027, reaching $215bn by 2030 from $30bn in 2025, with a 5-yr CAGR of 49%.

CoWoS tracker: fine tuning upwardly for total capacity 
Following our earlier raise in May Edition on May 12 in which we revised up TSMC’s CoWoS capacity 
from 175KPM to 195KPM by end-2027, the Street has moved up to our numbers , and we have fine 
tune our estimate to 200KPM by end-2027 on our Sept Edition on Sept 14.

2026/2027/2028, and ASE’s CoWoS capacity of 15KPM/45KPM/55KPM by end 2026/2027/2028.  
For fabless, we keep our CoWoS estimates largely unchanged for AI GPU/ASIC. For our accelerator 
model, we maintain +98% YoY 2027E, and for 2028E, we now expect it to reach the TAM of $1,000bn.  
In our ASIC Update report on Oct 13, we lowered our TPU units in 2028 to 10m, vs. 8.9m in 2027.

Upstream tech – Optical 
Strong demand for pluggable through 2028. 
We expect that the total demand for 800G/1.6T will reach 90mn/80mn in 2027, increased by 
80%/167%+, mainly driven by: 1) Nvidia, Google and AWS and other ASIC’s Accelerators’ demand 
are ramping up; 2) GPU/ASIC scale -out bandwidth continuously  increase drives increasing 
GPU/ASIC-to-optical module ratio; 3) We expect that TPU for 2027 will be 8.9m and Google fully 
transitions to large -scale clusters in 2026 and the scale -up layer will adopt optical interconnects. In 
addition, as we expect that CPO/NPO will start since 2H27, represents a pure incremental opportunity 
for optical interconnects supply chain, benefiting FAU, laser, shuffle box, connector and OE players, 
including LITE, GLW, TSEM, Sumitomo and Browave. We also like Marvell and SMTC, for the 
TIA/Driver used in NPO OE.  
Scale-out bandwidth increase driving optical-module deployment 
For NVIDIA, each Rubin GPU is equipped with two CX9 NIC chips, doubling the scale-out bandwidth 
compared with Blackwell. We further estimate that Rubin Ultra will adopt four CX9 chips per GPU, 
leading to another 2 times increase in scale-out bandwidth, continuing NVIDIA’s consistent bandwidth-
doubling trajectory. Accordingly, we expect the ratio of 1.6T optical modules has risen from 1:2.5 to 
1:5 in Rubin. From CoWoS perspective, we expect 5m/2m Blackwell/Rubin and 20m 1.6T in 2026. 
For Google, we believe that Goo gle fully transition to large -scale clusters in 2026 and the scale -up 
layer will adopt optical interconnects. We believe that in the scale-up domain, the ratio of TPU to 1.6T 
optical modules is approximately 1:1.5; In the scale-out domain, the ratio is around 1:2.5, suggesting 
the overall TPU-to-optics ratio is approximately 1:4 (1.6T equivalent).

Nvidia/CSPs’ Scale-out/up CPO/NPO  
Within NVIDIA’s next -gen NVL576 and broader CSPs’ AI infrastructure plans, CPO and NPO are 
developed side -by-side to expand GP U/ASIC interconnect bandwidth: CPO is the ideal long -term 
design for lower power and latency yet faces headwinds from complicated advanced packaging, and 
yield risks, while NPO could deliver most short -reach optical performance gains with far better 
manufacturability and deployment flexibility, making NPO a reliable near -term scaling path and CPO 
the ultimate end -state architecture. NPO partially removes module -side DSP and shifts signal 
conditioning and equalization to system designs, greatly lifting the value of TIAs and laser drivers that 
take over part of traditional DSP functions; we estimate each 3.2T optical engine contains tens of 
dollars worth of combined TIA and driver chips, a clear improvement over standard pluggable optics. 
Our shipment forecasts at 6.5m/42.4m NPO optical engines in CY27/28.  
Scale-up domain to increase by NVL576 to compensate lower memory: With lower memory spec, we 
expect Nvidia to increase the scale -up domain by optics to enhance performance. According to 
Nvidia’s GTC 2026, th e optical scale -up fabric allows Nvidia to expand Rubin Ultra to an NVL576 
scale-up. Regarding CPO and NPO which are being parallelly developed, we expect NPO to be a 
high reliability scaling path for NPO. We now expect its scale -up to adopt NPO in Rubin U ltra and 
Feynman platform and estimate OE shipments of Nvidia’s platform to be 6.0m/19.7m in 2027/2028.

### Page 25

Sector Report | Technology

Nvidia’s NPO OE to be 6.0m/19.7m in 2027/2028 
For Nvidia, we now expect its scale up to adopt NPO in Rubin Ultra and Feynman platform and 
estimate OE  shipments of Nvidia’s platform to be 6.0m/19.7m in 2027/2028, based on full NPO 
adoption at NVL576 launch given NPO maturity. Coupled with other CSP’s platform, we estimate total 
OE market to be 6.5m/42.4m in 2027/2028.  
TIA/Driver to benefit from NPO 
NPO partially removes module -side DSP and shifts signal conditioning and equalization to system 
designs, greatly lifting the value of TIAs and laser drivers that take over part of traditional DSP 
functions; we estimate each 3.2T optical engine contains tens o f dollars worth of combined TIA and 
driver chips, a clear improvement over standard pluggable optics.  
A key architectural shift under NPO is the partial removal of DSP functionality at the module level, with 
signal conditioning and equalization increasing ly redistributed into system -level design. This 
significantly elevates the importance of TIA (transimpedance amplifier) and laser driver components, 
which effectively replace a portion of traditional DSP value. We estimate that each 3.2T optical engine 
will embed high tens of dollars of combined TIA and driver content, representing a meaningful step -
up versus traditional pluggable optics architectures.  
From a competitive standpoint, we expect Marvell to remain the primary beneficiary of both NPO OE 
given i ts stronger positioning high speed TIA/Driver. Semtech is well positioned as a secondary 
beneficiary through its exposure to high-speed analog signal chain components, particularly TIAs and 
laser drivers, which scale directly with optical engine proliferat ion under NPO. We forecast 
6.5mn/42.4mn NPO OE shipments in CY27/28. Based on our estimated front-end content per OE and 
a 30% market share assumption for Semtech, we estimate its NPO revenue of $315/936mn. 
Stock Implication 
Generally speaking, scale-up CPO/NPO is an incremental opportunity for optical interconnect supply 
chain, as it aims to replace copper and has no impact on scale -out side. Additionally, scale up and 
scale out CPO solution share the similar suppliers. That said, we expect the key benefic iaries to be 
components such as FAU, CW laser, ELSFP, shuffle, PIC, TIA and Driver. We: 1) prefer Lumentum/ 
COHR due to the demand upside for CW laser, and also like local cw laser players; 2) expect Browave 
(3163 TT) to benefit, given the sizable contribu tion of CPO’s shuffle box considering ~55% market 
share, $5-6k ASP and a volume ramp in 2H26; 3) we also like TSEM, given CSP preferring NPO 
solution for their ASICs scale up interconnection. 4) expect GLW to benefit, given the second supplier 
of shuffle b ox and main supplier of MMC connector/fiber. 5) Marvell/Semtech will also benefit from 
CSP/Nvidia’s NPO solution, since TIA and driver will upgrade in the NPO Optical Engine.

### Page 28

Sector Report | Technology

Google’s 6D Torus & Beneficiaries.  
We expect Google will migrate its TPU interconnect from 3D to 6D torus, re -organizing the 64 -chip 
elemental block from 4× 4× 4 to 2× 2× 2× 2× 2× 2. External optical links per block rise from 96 to 384, with 
optical ports per chip scaling increase from 1:1.5 to 1:6, driving 4x coherent lite transceivers and OCS 
demand. The trajectory aligns with NVIDIA's CPO/NPO move. Both are interconnect scaling that 
expands direct chip-to-chip bandwidth and reduces hop count, with TPU via a 6D torus and NVIDIA 
via CPO/NPO for Scale-up. 
Google’s migration from 3D  to 6D Torus: We expect Google to migrate its TPU interconnect from a 
3D to a 6D torus topology, re-organizing the elemental 64-chip building block from a 4× 4× 4 3D Torus 
cube into a 2× 2× 2× 2× 2× 2 hyper-cube. In the current 3D design, each chip connects to 6 nearest 
neighbors across X/Y/Z axes and the cube exposes 16 optical ICI links on each of its 6 faces, totaling 
96 external optical links per cube (~1.5 optical ports per chip). Under the 6D design, each dimension 
has 64 external optical links (2⁶ chips, on e outward link per chip per dimension), for a total of 6 ×  64 
= 384 external optical links (~6 optical ports per chip). The per-chip optical port count thus scales from 
1.5 to 6. Combined with Scale -out at an estimated 1:2.5, this brings the aggregate TPU -to-port ratio 
to 1:8.5. 
Lower latency and closer to non -blocking Scale-up: For a 4,096 -chip superpod, a 3D torus (4× 4× 4) 
carries a worst-case diameter of 24 hops, whereas a 6D torus (2× 2× 2× 2× 2× 2) halves that to 12 hops 
(2× 6), directly compressing collective-operation and latency. Higher dimensionality also spreads traffic 
across 6 orthogonal paths, improving bisection bandwidth and moving closer to non-blocking chip-to-
chip communication. Critically, shortening each edge from 4 to 2 nodes eliminates deep mu lti-hop 
electrical paths inside the block: the architecture deliberately reduces intra -cube copper 
communication and pushes a far larger share of traffic onto optical fiber. 
Key beneficiaries: The exact TPU generation for the adoption of 6D torus and initi al penetration rate 
remain unclear. That said, whether NVIDIA moves toward CPO/NPO or Google expands from 3D to 
6D, both are Scale -up strategies aimed at enabling greater inter -chip communication. Key 
beneficiaries include the 2.4T Coherent Lite module and the OCS supply chain, such as MEMS-based 
optical switch vendors, PIC/laser/DSP suppliers, and fiber/cabling vendors supporting the larger 
reconfigurable lightwave fabric.

Risks 
1) Alternative technologies; 2) AI demand and capex slowdown; 3) IT demand slowdown; 4) product 
delays.

TAKING STOCK

The Reserve Bank of India (RBI) has adopted a 
multi-layered defence strategy to support the 
rupee this year.

This has entailed a combination of outright 
intervention in spot/ forwards, regulatory 
action, liquidity steps, administrative measures 
and moves to curb import -related dollar 
demand.

The rupee has appreciated by 1.8% against the 
dollar in the past five days since recent 
measures took effect, paring YTD depreciation 
to 5.5% . High oil prices, weakness in capital 
inflows, a firm dollar , tariffs/ geopolitical 
worries, energy , and risk -off sentiments have 
kept the INR under pressure since last year.

### Page 2

ECHOES OF 2013 WITH FEW 
DIFFERENCES

Comparisons with 2013 are apt, though 
the backdrop differs.  As for the 
catalysts, markets reacted to the US 
Fed’s taper announcement in 2013, 
which pushed US yields higher  and led 
global investors to reduce exposure to 
vulnerable EM economies. As a result, 
capital inflows weakened, exposing 
funding mismatch es for countries, 
including India, that were running wide 
current account deficits.

In 2026, markets face the spectre of 
elevated oil prices, geopolitical 
uncertainty, global competition for 
capital, a firm dollar ( DXY appreciated 
+3.8% YTD) , and US inflation/ deficits 
which have settled at levels much higher 
than pre -pandemic years. This has 
pushed up UST yields, and the US Fed 
kicked off its rate-hike cycle in Sep26.

Against this backdrop, the RBI is 
pursuing the same objective , i.e., to 
ensure rupee stability, but the nature of 
the battle differs. The defence strategy 
has shifted from ‘crisis management’ 
mode in 2013 , due to weaker domestic 
macroeconomic balances (a wide current 
account gap, high inflation, a lower 
reserve buffer, and wide fiscal deficits) , 
to a ‘terms-of-trade shock and impact of 
a firm dollar’ in 2026.

Flashpoints are  nonetheless similar, 
with reliance on portfolio financing , 
leaving the external account vulnerable 
to shifts in global risk sentiment. 
Portfolio outflows have emerged as the 
primary transmission channel through 
which external shocks have translated 
into INR depreciation pressure , 
compounded by high energy prices and a 
firm dollar.

FX PLAYBOOK

While the response framework is similar 
between 2013 and this year, the broad 
sequence and nature of macroprudential 
measures differ. This year’s multi-
layered rupee defence toolkit has 
included:

-Direct FX defence  by way of 
intervention in the spot and forwards 
market. As of Aug26, the net forward 
dollar liabilities ballooned to $200bn, 
rising by ~$63bn vs . the month before . 
Recent sell/buy FX swaps are expected 
to lower this stock in the coming months.

-Attracting dollar inflows  by way of 
special swap facilities for FCNR deposits, 
and offshore borrowings , apart from 
liberalising taxes around debt inflows,

### Page 3

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

amongst others. We discussed this here, 
here, here and here.

Taking a proactive stance, a portion of 
existing FX reserves could be earmarked 
against forward liabilities (from maturing 
FCNR deposits) , helping to mitigate 
concerns that deposit maturities or debt 
repayments could trigger a sharp
increase in dollar ⁠demand down the line.

-Domestic liquidity management. Bonds, 
to some degree, are paying the price for 
the authorities' efforts to defend the 
rupee against the additional headwind of 
weak global conditions.  INR liquidity 
surged on the back of the swap windows, 
prompting the RBI to undertake open 
market operations, VRRR auctions, sell -
buy swaps, among others. While these 
have been effective in absorbing 
balances, bond yields have drifted higher. 
10Y yields are at three-year highs and are 
expected to climb further  (DBSf: 7.40 -
7.50% by year-end).

-Moderate FX demand and speculation . 
Earlier in the year, the RBI had 
introduced change s to the banks’ net 
open position framework, and thereafter 
partially rolled back a few curbs. 
Prudential measures on FX exposures 
and hedging -related measures were 
announced in Oct26, which include d the 
introduction of a daily cash reserve with 
the RBI equal to 20% of the rupee 
equivalent of the notional value for INR -
linked derivative contracts exceeding 
$2mn used to hedge current account 
transactions, referred to as the Foreign 
Exchange Risk Reserve (FERR) . 
Subsequent clarifications provided 
operational details on where the FERR 
would be maintained and that it would be 
subject to audits.

Since 12 Oct, the RBI has been providing 
dollars via designated banks to the three 
state-owned oil -marketing companies 
(Indian Oil, BPCL, and HPCL) to meet the 
their daily dollar requirements, thereby 
diverting related spot-market demand.

On hedging-related matters, the limit for 
undertaking foreign exchange derivative 
transactions without proving an 
underlying commercial exposure was 
cut to $5mn from $100mn across all ADs 
and exchange -traded currency 
derivatives. Restrictions on Rebooking , 
i.e., a uthorized dealers are prohibited 
from allowing customers to rebook 
cancelled rupee -linked derivative 
contracts (though rollovers upon 
maturity remain permitted). Lastly, 
banks must obtain written confirmation 
from users that the same underlying 
exposure has not been hedged with 
another dealer.  Data from CCI , cited by

### Page 4

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            4

Bloomberg, pointed to importers’ 
forward dollar purchases rising 44% on 
the year to a monthly average of $64bn.

-Current account management . Efforts 
to contain structural FX outflows by way 
of higher duties and taxes on gold 
imports discourage non -essential 
purchases and production incentives to 
improve export competitiveness.

IMPACT

To assess whether the playbook has 
worked, we observe:

a) Rupee has enjoyed a brief respite. 
Despite oil prices at $105/bbl at the time 
of writing and elevated global yields, 
USDINR fell from near 96.80 to wards 
95.00, owing to measures announced in 
Oct26 following the central bank’s 25bp 
hike. Beyond temporary relief, global 
catalysts are likely to be back to the fore 
and weigh on the currency (see chart);

b) Sharp inflows under the swap 
facilities pushed the foreign reserves 
stock to a record high, providing 
policymakers sufficient buffer to 
navigate future shocks from a position of 
strength. A strong interventionist 
response has since led to a softening in 
the overall stock (see chart).

c) Macroprudential measures have led to 
distortions in other parts of the market . 
For instance, strong swap -related 
inflows had inorganically pushed 
onshore INR liquidity up sharply, 
necessitating absorption measures. 
Similarly, NOP restrictions and the more 
recent tighter requirements on hedging 
activity and counterparty transactions  
had some after -effects. Forward points 
have surged this week , with financial 
institutions pointing to relative illiquidity 
in the currency forwards market  on 
volumes and activity basis, and fewer 
avenues to deal with dollar supply.

### Page 5

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            5

d) Swap-related windows are a timely 
support for the country’s balance of 
payments position. From expectations of 
a third consecutive year of BOP deficit in 
FY27, the math has now swung to a 
potential surplus, even as current 
account worries remain. With energy 
prices settling north of $100/bbl, 
keeping the monthly trade deficit near 
$30bn, our FY27 current account deficit 
forecast of 1.1% of GDP faces  upside 
risks of around 0.3-0.5% of GDP. We note 
the s harp jump in ECB outflows in S ep, 
though see that as a temporary overshot 
of leverage-FCNR activity.

WHAT LIES AHEAD

Authorities are likely not done yet and 
remain ready to take further action, as 
required. RBI Governor Malhotra 
reiterated the authorities’ serious intent 
of stabilising the currency in a recent 
interview (Moneycontrol, 13 Oct), 
emphasising that “The RBI is determined 
to do whatever is necessary to ensure 
orderly movement in the foreign 
exchange market,”. He also highlighted 
that recent steps were not tantamount to 
capital control measures that were 
aimed at ensuring that forex markets 
serve genuine hedging needs rather than 
speculative bets.  Market participants 
opine that FX volumes and interest are 
likely to be subdued in a few pockets on 
the back of the recent measures, 
consequently stabilizing the rupee.

Unlike in 2013, when policy tightening 
was a part of the initial response, the 
2026 strategy has prioritised targeted 
FX and regulatory measures.  The 
sequencing suggests a deliberate effort 
to distinguish actions aimed at 
preserving currency stability from those 
intended to achieve domestic monetary 
objectives, with rates remaining a 
potential, though not immediate, tool in 
the defence framework.

However, global developments have 
altered that dynamic . Rising US yields 
and a gradual increase in the US Fed 
Funds rate (DBS baseline: a total of 75bp 
hikes) have compressed IN -US spreads. 
The recent sell -off in INR benchmark

### Page 6

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            6

bonds has effectively widened Gsec -
UST spreads across maturities, in 
contrast to the compression witnessed in 
regional high-yielders.

The latest announcements may not 
fully quell speculation over further 
policy action.

Upcoming measures might include -

-Markets anticipate relief on the long -
term capital gains tax structure on 
equities to draw in longer-duration 
inflows. This measure will boost inflows 
if it coincides with other factors that 
make domestic equities attractive for 
thematic investing , as global risk -free 
rates moderate.

-Street expectations also include steps 
to boost inward dollar repatriation by 
exporters to boost domestic dollar 
supply.

-Wider policy rate corridor akin to 2013.

-Policy rate response. While this might 
not be a panacea, it is a likely necessary 
condition. Raising the neutral rate might 
have mixed success in attracting capital 
market inflows ; though rate-sensitive 
flows are likely to get a fillip as investors 
seek attractive rate differentials in the 
region.

An adequate real rate cushion would 
also align with the evolving domestic 
inflation realities, which are expected to 
accelerate to 6% in the Oct-Dec quarter 
and remain above 5% well into 2027.

Given fluid global developments, we 
outline three scenarios on the rate path 
ahead. Our baseline assumption  is for 
75bp hikes in this cycle  (including Oct), 
spread between the Dece mber and 
February 2027 meetings. We don’t rule 
out an intermeeting rate hike before the 
December meeting, contingent on 
rupee movements.

Under the second scenario , s hould 
depreciation pressures on the rupee 
return, the 50bp hike might be brought 
forward to December , with another in 
February, taking cumulative hikes in this 
cycle to 100bp. The t hird scenario  is a 
more aggressive 150bp worth of hikes in 
this cycle, with 50bp front -loaded in 
December, and the rest spilling into 2027. 
Factors that  will determine whether 
these scenarios materialise are the 
resumption and scale of rupee 
depreciation, oil price direction, US Fed 
Funds rate increases (DBSf: total 75bps) 
and other risk-off events.

Path ahead

Despite these measures, we see limited 
room for sustained INR appreciation on 
grounds of manufacturing/ trade 
competitiveness. A challenging flows 
portfolio flows outlook leaves the d oor 
open to further gradual rupee 
depreciation subject to global 
developments, with high sensitivity to oil 
price movements and risk sentiments . A 
strong rate response will help introduce  
favourable rate differentials and reduce 
currency volatility.

## Document 408: US Equity Views_ Over-earning, but not an earnings bubble

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Equity Views_ Over-earning, but not an earnings bubble.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 18
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

semiconductor share prices have recently lagged the trajectory of near-term EPS
estimates. Similarly, the market trades at a P/E discount relative to what a macro model
would imply given the current level of corporate profitability. Our 12-month S&P 500

expanding valuations, will remain the primary driver of the bull market.

Goldman Sachs US Equity Views

### Page 3

The magnitude of S&P 500 “over-earning”

S&P 500 earnings grew by an extraordinary 51% year/year in Q2 2026 and have 
grown by 26% during the past four quarters. During the last 30 years, the recent pace 
of earnings growth has been exceeded only during the post-recession rebounds in 2010 
and 2021.

The recent strength of S&P 500 earnings growth has raised investor concerns that 
current earnings power is unsustainable and that the market is in an “earnings 
bubble.” S&P 500 earnings have recently outpaced both their historical trend and their 
historical relationship with US GDP growth. S&P 500 4-quarter EPS growth has averaged 
7% during the past 30 years, and the relationship between S&P 500 earnings and US 
economic growth in recent decades would have pointed to a similar rate of earnings 
growth during the last few quarters.

Exhibit 1: S&P 500 trailing 4-quarter EPS has grown by 26% during the past year

### Page 4

The degree to which the US equity market is currently “expensive” depends in large 
part on the sustainability of recent earnings strength. The exceptional recent 
strength of earnings has driven an unusual wedge between equity valuation multiples 
based on near-term earnings and valuations on trend earnings. Because equity prices 
have failed to keep pace with surging earnings, near-term valuations show no hint of a 
bubble: The S&P 500 12-month P/E ratio has declined from 23x a year ago to 19x today 
and now matches its 10-year average. However, even an “average” multiple may be 
expensive if current earnings are unsustainable. The cyclically-adjusted P/E based on 
trailing 10-year earnings has climbed to one of its highest levels on record, falling shy of 
the highs of 1999-2000 but surpassing the level reached in 2021. Similarly, our 
colleagues have argued that the increase in market value of AI companies during the last 
few years requires an optimistic estimate of the future magnitude of AI-related profits 
and who will capture them.

### Page 5

The AI boom and S&P 500 earnings growth

Affirming investor concerns that US companies are “over-earning,” there are three 
factors that are boosting earnings today but will contribute to deceleration going 
forward: 1) AI capex spending, 2) semiconductor margin expansion, and 3) earnings 
from private investment gains.

AI capex 
The AI investment boom is driving nearly half of S&P 500 EPS growth this year, but 
its contribution will fade going forward. The mega-cap US hyperscalers are on track to 
spend $800 billion on capex this year, an increase of 94% vs. 2025. That spending is 
flowing through to the earnings of the AI infrastructure complex including 
semiconductor, tech hardware, industrials, and utilities companies, which are collectively 
accounting for roughly half of consensus S&P 500 earnings growth this year. The AI 
boom is also having a secondary impact on earnings outside of the infrastructure 
complex, for example by boosting capital markets activity and consumer wealth. Both 
consensus and GS analyst forecasts show hyperscaler capex growing at a slower rate in 
coming years, which should result in decelerating earnings growth for much of the AI 
infrastructure complex and a fading tailwind to S&P 500 earnings.

### Page 6

Hyperscaler depreciation expenses will continue to increase as capex growth 
decelerates, further dampening the boost of AI investment spending to S&P 500 
earnings growth. We estimate a drag from hyperscaler depreciation expenses on S&P 
500 earnings growth of 5pp in 2027, offsetting nearly half of the 11pp boost to earnings 
from capex spending. By 2028, the drag from depreciation should offset the S&P 500 
earnings uplift from continued capex spending.

### Page 7

If capex were fully expensed, consensus 2027 S&P 500 EPS would be 22% lower than 
under current accounting rules. The current gap between actual reported and 
hypothetical fully-expensed earnings is one of the widest in recent decades, exceeded 
only as earnings fell more quickly than capex during the recessions of 2001 and 2008.

Hyperscaler capex has consistently surprised relative to consensus estimates during 
the last few years, and the potential for additional surprises going forward creates a 
wide range of potential S&P 500 earnings outcomes. Our equity analysts expect 
hyperscaler capex will total $1.2 trillion in 2027 and $1.4 trillion in 2028, in which case 
the current tailwind from AI capex to S&P 500 EPS will fade by 2028. Exhibit 10 below 
outlines upside and downside scenarios around that base case. Next year, a $250 billion 
surprise in hyperscaler capex — in either direction — would shift S&P 500 EPS growth by 
about 6 pp in the same direction.  
We estimate that hyperscaler capex would need to decline by roughly 30%, to $570 
billion, to offset earnings growth for the rest of the S&P 500 in 2027. This decline 
would reduce earnings for the AI infrastructure complex by about 40%, leaving their 
profits about 25% above 2025 levels.

### Page 8

Semiconductor margin expansion 
A sharp increase in profit margins for many semiconductor companies has 
contributed to the above-trend recent pace of S&P 500 earnings growth. The 
combination of strong demand and limited supply has boosted semiconductor prices 
and gross margins, with the most pronounced margin expansion among memory stocks. 
S&P 500 memory firms currently enjoy gross margins of roughly 80%, more than double 
their historical average. Across the semiconductor industry, gross margins sit at their 
highest level in decades, and consensus expects margins to remain at similar levels 
through 2028.

Margin expansion has accounted for a large share of recent semiconductor earnings 
growth, but that boost should fade going forward. We estimate that about 1/4 of S&P 
500 Semiconductor earnings growth in 2026 is being driven by gross margin expansion. 
Our equity analysts believe the balance of memory supply and demand is likely to 
remain tight through 2027, with incremental supply additions unlikely to impact industry 
margins until 2028 at the earliest. However, they expect the rate of incremental margin 
expansion to slow meaningfully next year, reducing the contribution of semiconductors

S&P 500 earnings growth in the next few years will be highly sensitive to the 
trajectory of semiconductor margins. In a scenario where slowing AI infrastructure 
investment, increasing supply, and/or technological shift lowers semiconductor prices 
and profit margins, S&P 500 EPS growth would also disappoint. We estimate that each 
percentage point change in S&P 500 semiconductor gross margins next year would shift 
S&P 500 EPS growth by about 1 pp.

### Page 10

“Other income” from private investment gains 
Appreciating equity investment stakes are also temporarily inflating S&P 500 
earnings. Mega-cap tech earnings have recently been boosted by large GAAP “other 
income” related to their equity investments. In Q2 2026, the mega-cap tech companies 
enjoyed unrealized investment gains in private companies that totaled roughly $150 
billion, translating into 12% of S&P 500 EPS. We expect H2 2026 reports will include 
additional “other income” that should be larger than the gains in Q1 but smaller than Q2 
totals. We expect a much smaller contribution in 2027. The complete removal of this 
“other income” next year would create a drag of 8 pp on S&P 500 earnings growth in 
2027 relative to 2026, all else equal.

### Page 11

S&P 500 earnings outlook

Our baseline forecast is for S&P 500 EPS to rise to $415 in 2027 (+11% year/year) 
and to $460 in 2028 (+11%). We expect an above-consensus base of earnings in 2026 
due to further “other income” gains from private equity stakes in H2 of this year. 
Excluding the “other income” inflating earnings in 2026, S&P 500 earnings growth next 
year would register 18%. Our 2027 EPS forecast is similar to the bottom-up consensus 
estimate of $419 but above the median top-down strategist estimate of $403.

### Page 12

Our 2028 EPS forecast is about 5% below the bottom-up consensus, signaling a 
return to the typical historical pattern of downward revisions to consensus earnings 
estimates after the last two years of rising estimates. Outside of the complex of AI 
capex beneficiaries, we expect sales growth will register close to nominal GDP , margins 
will expand modestly, and a growing boost from AI productivity will result in earnings 
growth of 9% in both 2027 and 2028.  Our forecasts for EPS growth among this group of 
companies are roughly 4-5% below consensus estimates in both 2027 and 2028, similar 
to the historical average magnitude of downward revision to consensus estimates. While 
AI investment spending has had a direct impact on the earnings of semiconductor and 
other AI infrastructure stocks, it has also lifted earnings for companies outside of the “ AI 
complex,” including by boosting capital markets activity as well as supporting consumer 
spending via the wealth effect. 
Solid GDP growth and easing pressure from energy prices will be the main macro 
tailwinds for earnings growth in 2027. Economic growth is the primary variable in our 
top-down earnings model and explains more than half of the historical variation in S&P 
500 EPS growth. Our economists expect US real GDP growth will average 2.1% in 2027 
and 2.3% in 2028. Our commodity strategists expect Brent crude oil will decline to 
$80/bbl by the end of 2027, which would weigh modestly on Energy sector profits but 
boost earnings for most other sectors. Outside of the AI-related risks outlined above, 
persistent input cost pressures and risks to the consumer from equity market volatility 
are two of the key macro downside risks to our earnings forecast.

### Page 13

As the earnings tailwind from AI investment spending fades, the trajectory of S&P 
500 profitability in coming years will depend increasingly on the realization of AI 
productivity gains. Our forecasts embed a 1 pp boost to S&P 500 EPS growth from AI 
productivity in 2027 and a 2 pp boost in 2028. Economy-wide surveys and corporate 
commentary signal enterprise adoption that is increasing but remains in early stages, 
and the impact of AI adoption on corporate earnings still appears narrow. However, the 
recent acceleration in enterprise AI spending suggests that the earnings impact of AI 
adoption should become clearer in coming quarters.

### Page 14

Valuing S&P 500 earnings

Like our forecasts and consensus estimates, market pricing suggests investors 
anticipate a deceleration in earnings growth going forward. This is true both for the 
broad market and for AI infrastructure stocks. For example, the share prices of 
semiconductor stocks have historically mirrored the path of consensus EPS estimates, 
and the recent moderation of semiconductor returns has aligned with consensus 
estimates for decelerating growth in 2027 and 2028.

The current S&P 500 P/E multiple signals an optimistic outlook but market 
skepticism regarding the sustainability of current profits. In addition to rising interest 
rates, an anticipated deceleration in earnings helps explain the recent decline in the S&P 
500 forward P/E multiple. Along with macro factors like interest rates, the level of 
corporate profitability has helped explain the variation in S&P 500 valuation over time. 
Based on the historical relationship between S&P 500 ROE and P/E, the current S&P 500 
ROE of 24% — a record high — would point to a forward 12-month P/E of over 21x 
against today’s macro backdrop. Given current inflation and interest rates, today’s S&P 
500 multiple of 19x would be consistent with an ROE of roughly 22%, more than 200 bp 
below the current level but otherwise matching 2021 as the highest on record.

### Page 15

Earnings, rather than valuations, should continue to drive equity market upside 
from here. Our 3-, 6-, and 12-month S&P 500 return targets are +5 (8000), +9% (8300), 
and +14% (8700). These imply a P/E multiple on consensus forward EPS that remains 
close to the current level of 19x. Our economists and interest rate strategists expect 
interest rates to decline modestly in coming quarters, creating some macro upside risk to 
equity valuations. However, we expect investor uncertainty regarding the impact of AI 
on the outlook for long-term profits to persist in coming quarters, making a dramatic 
increase in valuations unlikely.

## Document 445: 【國泰證期研究部】衛星產業-SpaceX D2C進展更進一步，星艦試射進展順利-20261016

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】衛星產業-SpaceX D2C進展更進一步，星艦試射進展順利-20261016.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 5

### Page 1

1 
產業更新 – 衛星產業 
SpaceX D2C 進展更進一步，星艦試射進展順利 
What’s New –  SpaceX 收購新頻譜，劍指行動通訊市場 
SpaceX於 10/8宣布收購 Grain Management旗下全美 800MHz低頻頻譜，涵蓋最多 14MHz
配對頻譜，交易金額估約 80 億美元。 800MHz 具備廣域覆蓋及室內穿透優勢，可與既有
2GHz頻譜互補，改善偏遠地區通訊品質。儘管仍待 FCC核准，惟主席 Brendan Carr 對新業
者投資持正面態度，預期有利交易推進。 
Trends/Outlook – 衛星、火箭升級短期影響衛星部屬速度 
3Q26 SpaceX共完成 22次 Starlink低軌衛星部署任務，合計發射 578顆衛星，季減 16.8%，
9M26累計部署 2,167顆。單季發射數量下滑主要受任務頻率減少影響，惟單次平均部署 26.3
顆，略高於 2Q26的 25.7顆。此外，SpaceX於 9月成功透過 Starship部署 26顆新一代 V3
衛星，顯示大型運載火箭逐步具備實際衛星部署能力。另一方面，Amazon Leo於 3Q26僅完
成 1次發射任務，部署 29顆衛星，季減 81.3%，主要受運載火箭供應及發射排程限制，後續
須觀察 Vulcan、Ariane 6 及 New Glenn 等新一代火箭投入商業發射的進度。SpaceX另規劃
於路易斯安那州興建全球第四座太空港，預計 2027年動工、2029年首飛，規劃設置逾 12座
發射塔，全面建成後目標每年支援數千次 Starship飛行任務。

Investment Summary 
1. 研究部認為，EchoStar交易解決了 Starlink下一代 D2C的頻譜與容量問題，而近期收購
Grain Management 旗下全美 800MHz低頻頻譜，則進一步補強低頻地面覆蓋資源。有
了自有頻譜資源，搭配未來新一代 D2C衛星酬載之升級，SpaceX 已逐步豐富期從衛星寬
頻業者，走向整合型行動通訊服務供應商的完整資源。 
2. 維持 SpaceX 26/27 年新增 4,000/6,000 顆衛星預期不變，儘管 3Q26 新增數略低於預期，
短期受到新世代衛星、火箭轉換，影響現階段 Falcon 9星鏈任務次數部屬，惟考量下一代 
V3 衛星試射順利，4Q26有望啟動部屬，單次 Starlink 低軌衛星發射數量上限將由 29 顆
大幅上升至  60 顆，將有益於未來衛星成長在加速 ，故持續看好天上衛星供應鏈如華通
(2313 TT)、昇達科(3491 TT)、燿華(2367 TT)後市發展。

2 
SpaceX 收購新頻譜，劍指行動通訊市場 
SpaceX於 10/8宣布與數位基礎建設投資公司 Grain Management達成協議，擬收購美國全國性
800MHz低頻頻譜牌照組合，涵蓋最多 14MHz配對頻譜，市場預估交易金額約 80億美元。由於
800MHz具備較佳的訊號穿透能力及廣域覆蓋優勢，可與既有 2GHz中頻頻譜形成互補，提升室內
及偏遠地區通訊品質。該收購案目前雖仍需要 FCC進行批准，不過 FCC主席 Brendan Carr目前已
表態，認為更多投資與服務業者加入，將有助於推動新一代連線技術發展，因此預期此次收購將順
利完成，未來將加速 Starlink Mobile整合衛星與地面行動網路，強化 D2D服務覆蓋能力，並逐步
降低對傳統電信商的依賴，為 SpaceX進軍美國行動通訊市場奠定基礎。 
SpaceX於 25年進行第一次的頻譜收購，向美國衛星及電信業者 EchoStar收購旗下無線通訊頻譜
使用權及相關資產，主要獲得 AWS-4 使用權之 2GHz頻譜，初步奠定了 SpaceX在行動衛星通訊
的基礎，且逐步掌握自主頻譜資源。然衛星通訊連線存在的痛點之一，便是經常因建築物、遮蔽物
等造成訊號傳遞出現障礙，因此過去 Starlink用戶終端天線皆需要安裝在水平面 15 度以上、天空
完全無遮蔽的屋頂或陽台上。而在新取得的低頻頻譜後，SpaceX 將具備傳輸距離遠、可穿透建築
物及樹木等障礙物的優勢，有助改善衛星行動通訊的室內訊號覆蓋能力，降低對傳統基地台的依賴。
另外目前大多數行動手機均支援 800MHz頻段，因此未來消費者若使用 Starlink Mobile服務，無
須另外購買專用衛星電話。 
圖一：Starlink Mobile 服務應用示意圖                                               圖二：D2C 衛星酬載 
資料來源：SpaceX、國泰證期研究部整理 
研究部認為，EchoStar交易解決了 Starlink下一代 D2C的頻譜與容量問題，而近期的收購則進一
步補強低頻地面覆蓋資源。有了自有頻譜資源，搭配未來新一代 D2C衛星酬載之升級，SpaceX 已
逐步豐富期從衛星寬頻業者，走向整合型行動通訊服務供應商的完整資源。

### Page 3

投資 顧問 
產業報告 
 2026/10/16

3 
新世代衛星、火箭升級短期影響衛星部屬速度 
3Q26 SpaceX共計完成 22次 Starlink低軌衛星部署任務，合計發射 598顆衛星，包含次軌道測試
衛星 20顆，季減 11.1%，年初累計至 9M26共部署 2,167顆。其中 7、8、9月分別部署 210、
261及 107顆，9月發射數量明顯下降，推測因 Starship試飛任務導致 Falcon 9 星鏈任務減少影
響，單季任務數由 2Q26的 27次降至 22次。然 3Q26單次任務平均部署衛星顆數約為 26.3顆，
略高於 2Q26的 25.7顆，顯示整體發射數量下滑主要來自任務頻率減少，而非單次載運數量下降。
此外，SpaceX於 9月 28日成功透過 Starship Flight 14 將 26顆新一代 Starlink V3 衛星送入軌
道，為 Starship首次完成正式衛星入軌部署。隨著下一代衛星導入更大型天線與高功率通訊酬載，
單顆衛星重量及通訊容量同步提升，目前短期可能限制 Falcon 9單次部署數量，但長期隨 Starship
發射能力逐步成熟，有望加速高容量 Starlink V3衛星部署，帶動整體星系寬頻容量持續擴張。 
此外，另一位衛星運營商 Amazon Leo 3Q26僅完成 1次低軌衛星發射任務，部署 29顆衛星，數
量大幅季減 81.3%，年初至今共部署 216顆衛星。3Q26發射任務於 7/2由 ULA Atlas V 火箭執
行，其後 8、9月均無新增衛星部署，顯示 Amazon Leo 衛星部署進度仍受到運載火箭供應及發射
排程限制。我們認為，Amazon Leo目前主要瓶頸在於發射運能，而非衛星製造能力，後續須觀察
包含 ULA Vulcan、Arianespace Ariane 6及 Blue Origin New Glenn 等新一代火箭逐步投入商業
發射，是否能夠帶動 Amazon Leo發射進程提升。 
圖三：Starbase Louisiana 示意圖 
資料來源：interstellar gateway、國泰證期研究部整理 
SpaceX 於今年 8 月拍板定案，將於美國路易斯安那州南部 Vermilion Parish興建全球第四座太空
發射基地（Starbase Louisiana） ，基地位於墨西哥灣沿岸，占地約12.5萬英畝（約 5.1萬公頃） ，
面積超過紐約曼哈頓 8倍，預計 2027年正式動工，並於 2029年執行首次 Starship發射任務。基
地規劃設置超過 12座發射塔，全面建成後目標每年支援數千次 Starship飛行任務。研究部預期，
隨著 Starship逐步實現高頻率發射與火箭重複使用，新增發射基地將有助於突破既有太空港的發射
排程及運能限制，進一步加速 Starlink下一代 V3衛星部署，並提升 SpaceX大規模建置低軌衛星
的能力。

《投資關鍵-澳洲貨幣政策取決地緣與油價變化》
澳聯儲主席布洛克在 2026年9月22日的最新講話中明
確表示，4.5%的失業率目前“仍然偏緊”，失業率需
要升至4.5%~5.0%之間，才能有效為勞動力市場降溫
並緩解通膨壓力；然隨後公布的 8月失業率由 4.5%升
至4.7%，已落在央行目標區間。不過澳洲8月份CPI數
據，年增由3.5%升至4.0%為澳央9月再度升息的主因 ，
主要受到能源價格漲所致；然整體結構未見失控風險 ，
核心CPI年增率持平於3.6%，扣除扣居住與交通CPI年
增由2.2%降至2.1%，因此12月份會期升息與否取決
於油價能否回落 ， 如 地 緣 趨 緩 配 合 失 業 率 維 持 在
4.5~5.0%區間，澳央12月有暫停升息空間。

另外，觀察CFTC投機頭寸顯示，澳幣投機空頭已經大
量累積，做空倉位正在主導市場 ，短期認爲澳幣應在
0.7~0.71的目標價盤整，更多上行空間應來自於美元
降息預期的重新開啓 ，或更多在明年中期左右 。我們
認爲澳幣的看法仍然中性略為正向，而近期跌破0.7關
卡的價位下，有些許布局空間。
• 我們認爲澳幣短線中性偏正向，
在澳幣短期有所回撤 0.7以下價
位下，可適時加碼澳幣部位。

《投資關鍵-美元強勢壓抑紐元表現 ，惟中期復甦動能
仍在》
9月份紐元表現主要受外部因素主導 。儘管紐西蘭經濟
數據顯示改善跡象，但油價上升及美國財政赤字憂慮推
動美國長天期公債殖利率升至逾二十年高位，帶動美元
走強及市場重新評估美國利率前景 ，令NZD/USD由月
初接近0.59水平回落至0.56附近，紐元與其他高貝他貨
幣同步受壓。
經濟基本面方面 ，紐西蘭第二季 GDP季增0.2%、年增
2.6%，優於央行預期 ，顯示經濟持續復甦 。企業投資

及AI相關無形資產支出保持穩健，惟家庭消費及零售活
動仍然偏弱，反映內需復甦步伐相對溫和。貨幣政策方
面，市場預期RBNZ將於10月維持官方現金利率不變，
以評估既有緊縮政策的影響；惟若經濟活動及通膨壓力
持續展現韌性，年底前不排除進一步收緊政策的可能。
在主要已開發國家央行政策路徑逐漸分化的背景下，紐
西蘭相對較高的利率水準仍可為紐元提供一定支持，但
短期市場焦點預料仍集中於美國利率及美元走勢。
展望未來一至兩季，我們認為紐元仍有望受惠於國內經
濟成長改善及投資活動回暖，逐步擺脫近期低位。然而，
在美國利率維持較高水準及美元強勢延續的環境下，紐
元上行空間可能受到一定抑制 。因此，我們雖仍預期
NZD/USD 可自目前水準溫和回升，但將第四季目標價
由0.60下調至0.59，以反映美元強勢環境可能維持較長
時間。

通膨數據回落，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要 《投資關鍵-政策觀望與美元強勢夾擊 ，加幣延續弱
勢格局》
中東局勢未見緩和，油價及通膨壓力維持高檔，促使
聯準會於9月16日升息25個基點，市場焦點亦由降息
預期轉向利差優勢及避險需求，導致非美貨幣承壓。
在市場風險情緒急轉直下，加幣自 USD/CAD 1.385
附近持續走弱，並一度來到 1.42以上。
加拿大央行料維持審慎觀望立場，市場對其轉向鷹派
的預期仍缺乏數據支持。儘管油價上升可能推升整體
通膨，但剔除能源因素後，加拿大經濟基本面尚未出
現足以支持進一步收緊政策的明顯改善。加上近期市
場已大幅回補加元空頭部位，在下一份就業報告公布
前，預料難有促使加拿大央行改變政策基調的催化劑，
因此貨幣政策前景仍偏向中性。
另一方面，高油價有助提升加拿大能源出口收入，對
加幣形成一定支撐；惟中東地緣壓力和美加貿易不確
定性猶存，料將拖慢加拿大經濟及就業復甦步伐。綜
合上述因素，我們預期加幣短期走勢仍偏疲弱，故將
4Q26美元兌加幣目標價由1.37上調至1.39。

《投資關鍵 –人民幣迎結匯需求與中美利差擴大的拉鋸》
人民幣與美元指數過去維持相關性，惟2025年下半年開
啟人民幣中期獨立升值行情。儘管兩者走勢高度相似，
但斜率拉開差距，人民幣升值動能顯著強於貶值方向。
今年以來中國景氣K型化樣態持續，出口景氣能見度高
於內需，累積大量貿易順差所引導結匯需求支持人民幣。
惟留意中國內需疲軟依舊，川習會落幕後，中國官方政
策焦點轉向穩增長與五中全會。除房市救市政策積極推
出外，中國人行宣布下調PSL 利率一碼至1.5%。中期來
看人民幣升值動能或受兩因素制約，首先中國經濟週期
挑戰加深不排除有資金流出壓力。其次，由於市場對美
國聯準會的升息預期加強，與中國寬鬆貨幣政策有所差
異，美中利差恐不利人民幣表態。
總結來看，4Q26人民幣將在結匯需求 、中美利差擴大

等因素拉鋸 ， 預 計 人 民 幣 走 勢 偏 向 溫 和 升 值 ，
USD/CNY Q4 6.65， 明年Q1 6.55。

### Page 6

《投資關鍵 -南非幣短線承壓，利差優勢支撐中期前
景》
南非幣近期承壓 ，美元兌南非幣由月初約 16.1升至
16.7水準，主要受美債殖利率攀升及美元轉強拖累 。
市場重新定價美國高利率將維持更久 ，帶動新興市
場資產普遍承壓 。此外，南非債市外資流出及避險
需求升溫 ，進一步加劇南非幣波動 ，使其表現落後
多數主要新興市場貨幣。
儘管外部環境轉趨逆風 ，南非國內經濟基本面仍展
現一定韌性 。近期零售銷售及消費數據表現穩健 ，
支持經濟溫和復甦 。雖然經常帳赤字 、外部需求疲
弱及油價上升、聖嬰現象（El Niño）帶來的糧食供
給風險 ，可能增加未來通膨壓力 ，但亦提高南非央
行維持偏鷹立場的必要性 。我們認為 ，在內需仍具
韌性及通膨風險升溫下 ，南非央行短期不急於轉向
寬鬆，較高利率環境可望持續提供南非幣利差支撐 。
短期而言 ，南非幣仍將受到美國利率 、美元走勢及
全球風險情緒主導 。若美債殖利率維持高檔 ，南非
幣可能持續面臨波動與資金流出壓力 。然而，相較
其他新興市場貨幣 ，我們對南非幣中期前景略趨正

面。一方面，南非央行偏鷹立場有助維持利差優勢；
另一方面 ，黃金價格回穩 、新興市場外匯波動率處
於相對低位 ，以及穩定的套息交易需求 ，均有利南
非幣表現。
重點摘要
